# MEKA OS build plan (the build board)

This file is the single source of truth for what is built, what is next and what is blocked. Unattended build runs
read it first and update it last. The owner reads it to see progress.

Status marks: `[x]` done · `[~]` in progress (partly landed) · `[ ]` not started · `[!]` blocked on the owner (see "Needs Meka").

## Rules every build run follows

1. **Hard constraints (never break, never "temporarily" relax):**
   - MEKA OS never holds trading or exchange credentials. Trading is event/approval based only.
   - Personal message sending is never more than "ask me, then send" (autonomy Level 3).
   - No unofficial WhatsApp client libraries. Messaging goes through the Android notification listener only.
   - Financial actions and trading approvals always need biometric confirmation.
   - Untrusted content (email, messages, web, news) never raises its own autonomy level (ADR-006).
2. **One item per run.** Take the first unchecked, unblocked item below. If it is too big for one run, land a
   working slice, mark it `[~]` and write what remains under the item.
3. **Main stays green.** Run `tools/core-verify.sh` and the relevant Gradle tests locally where possible, push, then
   watch CI. Pushes to `main` deploy the backend after CI passes. If CI fails, fix it in the same run or revert.
4. **Database migrations are additive only** (new tables/columns). Never drop or rewrite the owner's data.
5. **No new paid services, no new AWS resources that cost more than ~£2/month, no new third-party data sharing**
   without the owner's yes. Put the question under "Needs Meka" and skip to the next item.
6. **Animation is part of done.** Every new screen follows the motion catalogue below and honours reduced motion.
7. **Both apps.** A feature is done when it works on the Fold and the Mac (Mac may use a simpler layout).
8. **Dark is the default theme**; every screen must look right in Dark and Light.
9. **Commit messages** start with the milestone (`M1:`, `V1:`, `V2:`). Update this file in the same commit.
10. **Actions minutes are finite** (2,000 free standard minutes a month, then Meka's Actions budget, raised on
    2026-10-06 to about $40/month so the build can run around the clock; it stops at the limit). Docs-only commits
    don't trigger CI, and deploy only runs when a commit touches the server (backend/, infra/, core/, build files).
    Land at most **70 code pushes to `main` per UTC day** (count the day's `ci` runs with event `push` first; if 70
    already ran, stop with "Daily CI allowance used."). Runs go **back to back** (Meka, 2026-10-07): a run that landed
    something with CI green starts the next one with fire_trigger; the hourly schedule is the safety net. The `macos` job
    (about 10x the cost of Linux) runs weekly and on manual dispatch only, and build runs dispatch it **at most once a
    day, at night**: the first run at or after 21:00 UTC dispatches `ci` once if any commit since the last green
    dispatched/scheduled `ci` run touched `macos/`, `core/` or the build files. A Mac-touching item can be marked done
    before then ("Mac checked nightly"); if the nightly run is red, the next run fixes it first. If CI jobs sit queued
    with no runner, the allowance is used up: stop without pushing code; it resets on the 1st.
11. **Stop rule.** If something can't be undone and could reasonably go either way, don't do it: write it under
    "Needs Meka" and move on.

**Type weight (Meka, 2026-10-06):** calendar events and other context you don't act on use the regular `body` style;
the heavier `itemTitle` weight is for things you act on (tasks, habits, approvals). Meka found medium-weight event titles
too heavy on the Fold. **Fold review 2026-10-08:** task rows (Today, Needs you, the Calendar agenda) and booked-session rows
use `body` too; Up next's title is the one bold line.

## Motion catalogue (applies to every screen)

Tokens live in `design/tokens/tokens.json` (`motion`). Reduced motion = short cross-fade, no movement.

| Moment | Motion |
|---|---|
| App open | Greeting fades up, sections stagger in 40 ms apart |
| Theme change | Every colour blends across (`themeBlend`) |
| Complete a task | The accent ring sweeps once round from the top, the inside floods with the accent as it closes and the check strokes in, short leg first (`checkDraw`, 420 ms); then the row compresses and leaves; light haptic. Off: shown done at once |
| Up next changes | Card content cross-slides; old item settles into the list. Up next is the cover screen's card on every screen (UP NEXT, title, line, Done · Tomorrow · Open; chips press in with a light haptic) |
| Plan my day | Timeline blocks cascade in; Apply sends blocks into Today with a shared transition |
| Sync | Status dot breathes while syncing. Pull to sync (Today on the Fold): Today follows the finger past its top (rubber-banded), a brass ring fills with the pull and a tick haptic marks the point where letting go syncs; then the ring turns (one turn per 900 ms, at least one) while the content holds, a light haptic when done, and everything settles back on the expand spring. Mac: ⌘R / Sync Now / the command bar scale-fade the same ring in beside Search · Plan my day while it turns. Off: the ring fills and holds still |
| Switch section (shell) | Content slides an eighth of the width the way you moved (Mac: pushes along the sidebar); lit pill springs across; tick haptic; the tab icons (a sun on the horizon, "!" in a circle, a calendar, a speech bubble) morph outline → filled on the approve spring, the brass flood growing from half the shape while the icon swells to 1.15 and settles (the one going quiet empties the same way). Mac: the sidebar's SF Symbols swap to their filled variant with the system's replace transition and a bounce. Off: the fill cross-fades, no swell |
| Sheets and panes | Spring up from the bottom as a sheet (Dark: rounded top corners, a sliver of the screen above; Light: flush and square at the top, so no grey band shows under the status bar) while the screen behind dims to 50 % black (phone) / scale-fade (Mac, the system sheet); the dim fades out as the pane drops away; Fold unfold morphs list→two-pane. Reduced motion: cross-fades, the dim still fades |
| Habit tick | The ring sweeps round, fills and the check strokes in (`checkDraw`, like completing a task) as the circle pops with a spring (only when ticked on screen; already ticked shows done; unticking returns to the outline at once), streak number rolls; week dots fill; light haptic |
| Fasting | Ring sweeps continuously (one-second linear glide); soft pulsing glow at goal; the 7 day bars fill on appear; light haptic on start/end; the history's heat strip fades in a week at a time, left to right, 40 ms apart, and History unfolds its rows in place; when an extended fast reaches its goal "You did it" pops in (spring) and the ring bursts once (light spreading out with twelve rays, light haptic). "Pick a day and time" unfolds in place (day chips, ‹ › steps the time by half hours with a tick haptic, the digits and the goal line cross-fade; Mac: a popover the system scale-fades). Reduced motion: the ring steps each second, steady glow, the strip shown at once, the line fades in with no burst |
| Goals | Progress bars fill on appear |
| Gym (booked sessions) | Today's session card rises into Today with the up-next group; as it moves on (booked → on now → "Did you go?" → went / rebooked) its content cross-slides; Went draws its check in (ring, fill, stroke) as it pops (spring) with a light haptic and the note field unfolds; Didn't go and Undo give a tick haptic. Goals: "Book my sessions" and Rotation chips blend (Mac: a switch and a menu). Went / Didn't go on the "Did you go?" notification: the system's own motion as it turns into the quiet note. The session's row in Today's timeline glides into its place like an event's and leaves once it's over; on the "now" card (cover screen, Mac menu bar) the session cross-slides in like an event and Went / Didn't go press in (0.97) with a light / tick haptic. "Open Hevy ↗" on the card gives a light haptic and hands over to the app; in the habit's settings the line under the workout-app field cross-fades (saved, or "That isn't a web address" with a tick haptic). Reduced motion: cross-fades, no pop |
| Weekly review | Numbers count up; tiles and sections stagger in; goal bars fill on appear; ‹ › slide the week across the way you moved with a tick haptic (Mac: push, ⌘[ ⌘]); Done reviewing pops a check (spring) with a light haptic. The Sunday-evening card rises into Today like the brief's and opens Review with the shell's slide. Reduced motion: cross-fades, numbers shown at once |
| Approvals | Swipe to approve with spring; card slides away; fingerprint success morphs to a check; medium haptic |
| Needs you stack | The top card follows the finger and tilts; the move it leans to (Done · Tomorrow · Open) fades in and pops at the threshold with a tick haptic; letting go flies it off the way it went with a light haptic and the next card rises from the pile (two peek out behind); Open springs it back and the task opens; the undo bar rises. Mac: the same with a drag or ← → ↑. Reduced motion: no tilt or flight, cross-fades |
| Request cards (Needs you) | Cards from people Meka watches sit above the stack and stagger in after the after-work card; Add and Change press in (0.97) with a light haptic, Not a task with a tick; the card folds away (the list's item motion; Mac: expand spring) and the undo bar rises with what was done ("Added “Pick up dry cleaning” · Tomorrow", "Thu 15 Oct: work from home"); Change then springs Search up with the new task's detail over the results (Mac: the Search sheet scale-fades with it beside the results). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Email triage | Items sort into their groups with a stagger |
| After-work summary | The card sits above Needs you's stack; the pane springs up (Mac: sheet scale-fades); people sort into place with a stagger, urgent first; a person unfolds everything they sent in place (expand spring; Mac: tick haptic); Done gives a light haptic and the pane drops away. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Assistant | Ask's field asks MEKA with Return (Fold and Mac); the question shows, then a thinking shimmer (skeleton rows); the answer's lines fade in one after another (the stagger) and up to three cards rise under them; a card's button gives a light haptic, the card folds away (expand spring) and the undo bar rises with what it did ("Added “Milk” · Undo"); Undo brings the card back. The "MEKA's AI · On · $1.20 of $20 this month" line cross-fades, lit in the accent colour when it needs a look. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Talk to MEKA (voice orb) | The mic beside Ask's field (a still brass ring with a microphone) starts talking with a light haptic; the panel unfolds under the field (expand spring) with the orb: it breathes while thinking (the empty states' breath), swells up to 1.22× with Meka's voice while listening (level up fast, down slowly) with a brass fill, and sends three rippling rings out while MEKA speaks (1.4 s each); its line ("Listening…" · "Thinking…" · "Tap to interrupt") cross-fades; the live transcript types in under it; tapping the orb gives a tick haptic (interrupts while speaking, else ends) and the panel folds away; a spoken yes folds the cards away and the undo bar rises as for a tap. Mac: the same orb beside the field (⌥Space starts it), clicking it interrupts or ends, the panel unfolds with the expand spring. Reduced motion: a still ring with a level bar, cross-fades |
| Capture | Field expands from the pill; captured item flies into the list. Capture sheet (share/widget/tile): springs up from the bottom, a check pops on Add and the sheet drops away; light haptic |
| Repeat and steps | Repeat picker unfolds from its row (Mac: menu); Skip/Tomorrow leave the list like a completion; a step tick draws its check the same way and pops with a spring, with a light haptic |
| Lists | Tab pill springs across (Mac: segmented control); a row unfolds its actions in place; Got it / Do it now leave like a completion with a light haptic; due chases and reviews are lit in the accent colour |
| Evening shutdown | Card rises into Today when the evening starts; sections stagger in; a tick completes like a task (ring, check, light haptic) and the row leaves; carried rows leave the list and glide into Tomorrow while its summary rolls over; Shut down pops a check (spring) with a light haptic and the pane drops away (Mac: sheet) |
| Renewals | Like Lists: rows unfold their chips in place; "Renewed"/"Paid" on a repeating one slides its date line up to the next date (any list row's changed date does the same) and the row settles into its new place with a light haptic; a one-off leaves like a completion; what needs doing is lit in the accent colour. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| News ticker (Today) | Picture-and-headline cards drift right to left at a constant ~40 dp/s in a seamless loop, fading out at both ends; the match card leads in Barça's colour and its line cross-fades; a finger (Mac: hover) holds it, dragging scrubs; calm mode drifts twice each time Today appears then rests, and ▸ fades in to set it going again (tick haptic); a story springs the News pane up on it (Mac: the sheet scale-fades). Appearance → News ticker: chips blend (Mac: segmented control) and the line cross-fades. Reduced motion: no drift, one still card with ‹ › paging (tick haptic), cross-fades |
| Floating ticker (Mac, View menu) | The strip fades in over every app and drifts like Today's (always moving); hover holds it; the grip drags it and letting go springs it onto the nearer edge (top or bottom) with a light haptic; a story brings MEKA forward with the News sheet scale-fading onto it. Reduced motion: placed at once, one still card with ‹ › paging, fades only |
| News widget (Fold home screen) | The launcher flips the picture-and-headline cards every 5 s: the next card drifts in from the right and fades up as the old one drifts off left (the ticker's direction); it flips only while the home screen is showing; a tap brings MEKA up on Today with the News pane springing up on that story. Reduced motion (the phone's animations off): one still card, ‹ › page |
| News widget (Mac desktop) | None of its own: macOS desktop widgets can't animate; the system cross-fades it when MEKA writes new stories. Clicking a story brings MEKA forward with the News sheet scale-fading onto it |
| News (Ask → More → News) | The row's label travels into the title; Topics unfolds the chips in place, a chosen chip's colour blends with a tick haptic (Mac: Topics menu); lanes stagger in 40 ms apart; a story's detail springs up from the bottom (Mac: pushes across inside the sheet); Previous/Next (swipe sideways; Mac ← →) slide the story the way you moved with a tick haptic; the matchday line cross-fades as kick-off nears; from the command centre a story springs the News pane up over Today (Mac: the sheet scale-fades onto that story). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Morning brief | Card rises into Today when the morning starts (the end of quiet hours) and goes at noon or once read; sections stagger in; chases due today and what needs doing are lit in the accent colour; headlines stagger in last; Topics unfolds the topic chips in place, a chosen chip's colour blends across with a tick haptic (Mac: a Topics menu); Got it pops a check (spring) with a light haptic and the pane drops away (Mac: sheet). The card's line (weather · the day · the next match) cross-fades as it changes. ▶ Listen under the date presses in with a light haptic and its label cross-fades to "■ Stop" while MEKA reads (Stop: tick haptic); after the wake alarm it reads with no press. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Notifications | Sections stagger in; a chosen chip's colour blends across (Mac: segmented control and menus); quiet-hour times unfold when turned on; the "what happens next" lines cross-fade as settings change. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Search | The pane springs up with the field focused (Mac: ⌘F sheet); groups stagger in 40 ms apart and rows glide as results narrow while typing; an open task's detail springs up over the results (Mac: pushes across beside them); a done task unfolds Reopen in place; a list item, goal or habit opens its tab with the shell's slide and its row unfolded. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Today timeline | Rows glide to their new places as the day moves on (the quiet Work band too, which leaves once work is over) (a finished event slips into "3 earlier", the now line moves down); "3 earlier" unfolds the finished events in place with its chevron turning; the now line's dot breathes gently and the free time from now sits on it ("1 h free until Work"), cross-fading as it changes; the Up next event line fades in an hour before. Reduced motion: cross-fades, steady dot |
| Event detail | Tapping an event (a row, an all-day chip, Up next's event line) springs its pane up from the bottom (Mac: sheet scale-fades); sections stagger in 40 ms apart; Join gives a light haptic; the status line ("In 25 min") cross-fades as time moves on. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Calendar | Day pills slide between weeks (swipe on the Fold; ‹ › push on the Mac); the lit pill's colour blends across with a tick haptic; tapping a day springs the agenda to it (the expand spring; from far away it jumps to a few rows short and springs the rest); agenda sections stagger in 40 ms apart and rows glide as the day moves on; a day's "+2 more" unfolds its other all-day rows with the list's item motion (Mac: expand spring); the calendar key fades in with the summary; the now line's dot breathes. Reduced motion: jumps and week changes at once, cross-fades, steady dot |
| App update | The "MEKA update ready" card rises into Today like the brief's; its line cross-fades between states; Install gives a light haptic and the bar fills as the build downloads; then Android's own Install prompt. Mac: the publish sheet scale-fades, a shimmer runs while it uploads, Published pops a check (spring) with a light haptic. Reduced motion: the bar jumps, cross-fades |
| Export (your data) | Sections stagger in; a shimmer runs while the file is built; once saved a check pops (spring) with a light haptic and the file's name; the pane springs up from Today's header (Mac: sheet scale-fades). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Talk (Ask → More) | The row's title travels into the pane as it springs up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); sections stagger in 40 ms apart (Side button · Headphones · Safety; Mac: On the Mac · Safety); the side button's status line blends to the accent once MEKA is the digital assistant (re-read when MEKA comes back from Settings); Open default apps presses in with a tick haptic. The mic on the bedside clock and at the end of Today's capture bar (Mac: the capture field's orb, press style) presses in (0.97) with a light haptic (dimmed with quiet hours at the bedside); at the bedside the Talk pane springs up over the clock with the orb listening and drops away on ‹ Clock; from the capture bar Ask opens with the shell's slide. Holding the side key or the headphones' button, tapping the Talk widget, or opening MEKA from the launcher with Bluetooth audio or in the car opens Ask with the shell's slide and the Talk panel unfolding as for the mic (light haptic). The Talk widget itself: the launcher's own motion. "When I open MEKA": Turn on / Turn off presses in with a tick haptic and the status line blends to the accent while on; with it on, a launcher open slides to Ask, a quiet chime, and the Talk panel unfolds listening; "Too noisy — tap to talk" fades in under the resting orb (accent) instead when the room is loud; nothing said in 6 s and the panel folds away and Today slides back. Mac: the same in the Talk sheet (press style, the line blends; sections On the Mac · When I open MEKA · Safety); a launch or a Dock click from the background pushes to Ask, Tink, and the panel unfolds with the expand spring; "Too noisy — click to talk" (accent) under the resting orb, a click starts listening. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| MEKA's voice (Ask → More) | The row's title travels into the pane as it springs up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); a shimmer while the server answers; the voices stagger in 40 ms apart; choosing one blends its dot and border to the accent with a tick haptic; ▶ Sample presses in with a light haptic and its label cross-fades to "■ Stop" while it plays (Stop: tick haptic); the status and month lines cross-fade; the device's own voices (Automatic, then each installed voice) stagger in after them the same way, "+3 more" unfolds the rest (staggered), and the Speed and Pitch sliders' lines cross-fade as the thumb steps, a tick haptic per step. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Activity | The pane springs up from Today's header (Mac: sheet scale-fades); day sections stagger in 40 ms apart; Undo gives a light haptic and the row's line cross-fades to "Undone at 09:12"; MEKA's voice line (the month's characters) fades in once the server answers. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Ask and More | Title, field and More's sections (Places · Daily · Settings) stagger in 40 ms apart, each a step after the one before began, its label leading its rows; the field is one pill with the mic inside its right end: typing unfolds its best matches under it (expand spring; Mac: opacity and a short drop), staggered, gliding as they narrow, and a task match or "See all 12 matches" springs the search pane up (Mac: ⌘F sheet) with a tick haptic (a match: light); a place in More opens with the shell's forward slide, its label travelling into the place's title, and "‹ Ask"/back slides it away (the title flies back to its row); Today's brief and shutdown cards carry their titles into their panes and the review card into the Review tab; Mac cards and rows press in (0.97) before their sheet or section appears; panes spring up over Ask; a lit Lists line blends its colour; Mac rows lift 2 pt on hover. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Command centre (open Fold, wide Mac window) | Unfolding grows the columns beside Today out from the right edge (the pane morph) while Today narrows and keeps its scroll; Needs you and Coming up stagger in 40 ms apart; opening a task cross-fades its detail into Needs you's place and Close/back cross-fades Needs you back; the stack keeps its own swipe motion. Mac: columns appear with the window's width, the same cross-fade. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Now card (closed Fold's cover screen, Mac menu bar) | When the thing changes the card's content cross-slides like Up next (old out left, new in from the right); the label's colour blends to the accent when something is starting; chips press in (0.97) with a light haptic; Done and Tomorrow let the card slide on to what's next. Mac: the content pushes in from the right, buttons use the press style. Reduced motion: cross-fades, no press scale |
| Bedside clock (Fold half folded on a table) | The app cross-fades to the clock; the time fades up, the date, alarm and section stagger in 40 ms apart; each minute only the changed digits roll up; the lower section cross-fades as the day moves on; tapping it springs up the brief or shutdown pane with a tick haptic; the news strip at its foot drifts at half Today's speed, dimmed, and fades away in quiet hours (a story springs the News pane up); quiet hours blend the colours down (`themeBlend`); opening flat cross-fades back. Beside the time the watch face (larger, up to 260 dp, a heavier 8 dp rim) draws itself in with the clock (the quick draw: rim, markers, arcs, hands sweeping from 12) and lives like Today's with a slower breath (8 s); in quiet hours it dims to 45 % with the colours and its second hand, breath and shimmer stop (redrawn each minute, so the hour and minute hands keep time); tapping it presses in with a tick haptic and springs the whole 24-hour Day ring up over the clock ("‹ Clock" or Back drops it away; it lifts quiet hours' dimming while up). Reduced motion: cross-fades, digits change in place, the face drawn at once, the hands still keeping time |
| Call assistant (Work screen) | The status line cross-fades as the switch, permission and work mode change; "Let MEKA screen calls" unfolds with the expand spring; the switch gives a tick haptic. Mac: a switch with the same line cross-fading. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Command bar (Mac ⌘K) | The bar scale-fades in from 0.96 over a dimmed window; rows glide as results narrow while typing; the lit row's highlight glides between rows (↑ ↓ or hover); running a command gives a tick haptic and the bar drops away. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Motion setting (Appearance → Motion) | Expressive (default): entrances rise 28 dp, 60 ms apart, growing from 0.96 and settling on a slower appear spring (about 1.4× longer; Today's sections, every staggered list and the cross-fading lines); complete/approve springs bouncier; count-ups 900 ms. Subtle: the original values. Off: cross-fades only. The chips blend with a tick haptic (Mac: segmented control) and the line cross-fades; nothing chosen plays Expressive with its chip lit (the phone's animation scale never stills MEKA). Motion check under it: its result line cross-fades as the choice changes and, when MEKA's Motion is Off, is lit in the accent (a tap gives a tick haptic and turns Expressive on); Play the opening (light haptic) goes back to Today with the shell's slide and the opening replays (Off: cross-fades) |
| Press and hover (everywhere) | Anything tappable presses in to 0.97 on the complete spring while held, with a faint veil, and springs back when let go (a quick tap still shows the whole press). Mac: every plain button does the same, and cards (Today's brief, shutdown, review, after-work and Up next cards, More rows) lift 2 pt under the pointer. Off: a brief dim, no scale or lift |
| Empty states | When Today is all clear ("You're clear.") or nothing is waiting in Needs you (full screen; Mac: the Needs you section), a small brass ring with a soft glow sits beside the line and breathes slowly in and out (one breath per 4.2 s, growing from 0.92 to full size). Needs you's empty page and the open Fold's / wide Mac's column then show Habits today · Waiting on · Coming up to renew, each section staggering in after the line; a habit's tick ring sweeps, fills and draws its check (`checkDraw`) with a light haptic, rows glide as they change, and a Waiting on or renewal row opens Lists with the shell's slide. Off: still, cross-fades |
| Opening moment (Day ring) | Under Today's header (not on the cover screen): the first open of the day, the brass mark (the dial's track) draws itself round from the top (`dayRingMark`, 600 ms); halfway, the day's arcs (events, planned tasks dimmer, booked sessions; finished ones dimmest) grow clockwise one after another, the stagger apart (`dayRingArc`, 360 ms each), while the now needle sweeps from midnight to now (`dayRingNeedle`, 720 ms); once the mark closes the centre counts up to "3 h 45 free · 4 to do" and the live tiles under the dial (next event, fast, habits, renewals) fade and rise in the stagger apart while their numbers count up with it. On that first open the greeting's letters also fade in one after another (`greetingLetter` 28 ms apart, each over `greetingLetterFade` 260 ms, the line rising 6 dp with the first). Expressive runs the mark, arcs and needle 1.4× longer (840 / 504 / 1008 ms), and on this first open the hour marks (a tick at 00 · 06 · 12 · 18, a fine dot at every other hour) fade in one by one behind the drawing mark. Later opens that day, and every return to Today after 15 s or more away (`DayRingRules.onReturn`), draw the ring in again: the mark, hour marks and needle round together over `dayRingQuick` (900 ms Expressive, 500 ms Subtle), the arcs and tiles growing over its last three quarters as the centre counts up, with Today's stagger replayed and the greeting as a whole; a shorter trip (a fingerprint prompt) leaves Today as it was. It plays once per open, not again when scrolled back. Off: drawn at once |
| Watch face (Today's header) | Fold review 2026-10-09 07:26, item 2: Today's header shows a 12-hour watch face in place of the 24-hour dial (150 dp open Fold and Mac, 96 dp closed Fold). The opening draws it in like the Day ring: the brass rim draws round from 12 (`dayRingMark`), the 12 markers (no numerals; 12 · 3 · 6 · 9 heavier) fade in behind it, the next 12 hours' arcs grow clockwise one after another (`dayRingArc`) and the gold tapered hour and minute hands sweep round from 12 to the time (`dayRingNeedle`). Then it lives: the rim breathes 60 % → 100 % with its soft blur, the hour's shimmer runs round it, what's on now glows with the breath, and the fine second hand sweeps with its comet tail; the hour and minute hands keep time every frame (power saving and Off: redrawn each minute, no second hand or breath). Tapping it presses in (0.97) with a tick haptic and springs the full 24-hour Day ring up as a sheet (Mac: the sheet scale-fades), where the ring draws itself in quickly and an arc opens its event or task once the sheet has gone. Reduced motion: drawn at once, the hands still keep time |
| Living Today (Day ring, always on) | Once the opening lands the ring stays alive like a mechanical watch: a fine brass second hand with a soft comet tail sweeps round once a minute (smooth, never ticking; fades in over 500 ms), the brass edge breathes 60 % → 100 % over 5 s with a soft glow reaching 8 dp out (the track itself a 3 dp brass line at 55 %; the hand's tail 60°), a band of light runs once round in the first 1.8 s of each hour, and the now dot pops on a damped spring as each minute turns (`DayRingLive`). The ring carries the day: work hours a faint band on the track (a shade brighter while at work), the gym's booked sessions a wider arc, the arc on now glowing with the edge's breath (a soft halo), a running fast an inner arc from its start round to its goal that fills with the centre's count-up; tapping an arc (Mac: clicking) opens the event or task with a tick haptic. Once the day is shut down the ring looks ahead: the centre reads "Tomorrow 09:30" · "Standup" (or "Nothing booked yet") and a hollow brass mark sits on the track at that time, coming up with the mark. Drawn on its own layer: a frame redraws only that layer, never recomposes Today; frames only while the ring is on screen and MEKA is in front. Power saving / Low Power Mode: no hand or breath, redrawn once a minute. The hand fades in again each time the ring has drawn itself in on a return. Off: a still edge, no hand |
| Swipe actions | A swiped row (an event: Prep task → / ← Hide from my day) shows the action's colour as soon as it moves (accent for adding, grey for putting away), a light wash deepening to full at the arm point (96 dp); the icon (+, an eye struck through) and label fade in, the icon growing from 0.6 with the swipe and popping (spring) to 1.25 at the arm point with a tick haptic; letting go does it with a light haptic and the row springs back. Needs you's card takes on up to 18 % of the move's colour as it leans (accent for Done, grey for Tomorrow/Later and Open) and the move's chip carries its icon (✓, a clock, ↑) growing and popping the same way. Mac: an event row's hover buttons carry the icons and spring in from 0.6 (approve spring); the stack's card washes and its chip's icon pops as on the Fold. Off: the colour still deepens, icons full size, no pop |
| Task detail | Opening a task from a row (Today's rows and Up next; Needs you's card on Open) on the closed Fold grows the detail out of that row: the container's edges and corners travel from the row to the sheet on the expand spring while Today dims, the content fades in from 20 % to 60 % of the way and the title travels across; closing shrinks it back into the row (or where it was). Opened from search, a notification or the cover screen: the sheet springs up as before. Mac: a task opened from a row grows out of it across the detail column (the same numbers), else pushes across. Rows stagger in 40 ms apart (When · Remind me · Repeat · Notes · Steps · Goal · actions); When and Remind me unfold in place with the expand spring; a chosen day chip's colour blends with a tick haptic; ‹ › on the time tick and the digits cross-fade; the row's label cross-fades as it changes; pills press in; Delete raises the undo bar. Mac: the same, Pick a date… is a popover the system scale-fades. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Quick alarms and timers | Typed into capture ("alarm 6:30", "timer 20 min"): the undo bar rises with "Timer set · 20 min · ends 14:52" and a light haptic (Mac: the same bar); the row joins Today under Up next with the expand spring, its line cross-fading as the minutes go; ✕ gives a tick haptic and the row leaves with the expand spring. Ringing: the wake alarm's screen (glowing slide to dismiss) on the Fold, a notification on the Mac. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Leave-by alarm | In the event detail (Fold pane, Mac sheet), once Leave by has a travel time "Ring as an alarm" unfolds under it with the expand spring; its On/Off pill's colour blends with a tick haptic (Mac: a switch) and the line under it cross-fades. Ringing: the wake alarm's screen (glowing slide to dismiss) on the Fold, a notification on the Mac. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Add event (Calendar) | "+ Add event" beside the Calendar title (only while an account allows editing; tick haptic) springs the pane up from the bottom (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); rows stagger in 40 ms apart (title focused · When · time and length · Calendar · Place · Notes · Add); day, All day and length chips blend their colour with a tick haptic; ‹ › tick and the digits cross-fade, as does the summary line ("Fri 9 Oct · 15:00–16:00"); typing when-words into the title ("Dentist Fri 3pm") moves the chips and digits the same way, and "Saves as “Dentist”" with "Keep “Fri 3pm” in the title" unfolds under the summary (expand spring; Mac: fades in); Add gives a light haptic, the pane drops away and the undo bar rises ("Adding “Dentist” to Google · Undo", five seconds); the line under Calendar's summary cross-fades as it is sent ("Added “Dentist” to Google"; a clash or refusal lit in the accent colour). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Edit event (event detail) | "Edit" (only where the account allows editing; tick haptic) cross-fades the detail into the Add event form filled in from the event (Mac: inside the same sheet); its rows stagger in as when adding; Save gives a light haptic, the pane drops away and the undo bar rises ("Moving “Dentist” in Google · Undo"); "Delete from Google" is a quiet red line with the same undo bar (no dialog); the event's edit line in the detail cross-fades as it is sent; "Delete anyway" (guests) is the same quiet red line. The change shows at once, before Google answers: a moved event's row glides to its new slot in Today and the agenda (the list's item motion), a deleted one collapses, an added one rises in its place (and is swapped for the real one without a jump once Google has it). Reduced motion: cross-fades |

Reduced motion: cross-fades |

Ringing (Fold, full screen over the lock screen): the brass thumb of "Slide to dismiss" glows and breathes (the empty states' breath), follows the finger, springs back short of 85 % and runs home past it with a light haptic; the label fades as it goes; Snooze presses in with a tick haptic; the snooze line cross-fades. - [x] Hands-free phone updates (Meka approved 2026-10-07 23:09): after every green CI run on main that touched `android/` or `core/`, GitHub builds the debug APK and publishes it, so the Fold offers "MEKA update ready" with no Mac involved. **Signing:** the Mac's debug keystore is in the GitHub Actions secret `MEKA_FOLD_SIGNING_KEY` (base64; Meka adds it with "Copy phone signing key.command"; password/alias are the debug defaults `android`/`androiddebugkey`); the workflow decodes it to a temp file, signs, and deletes it; never echoed or uploaded as an artifact. If the secret is missing the job skips with a notice (not a failure). versionCode stays the commit count, so CI and Mac builds never clash (same commit = same build; the server already refuses a build that isn't newer). **Publishing identity (security):** a dedicated release-only publisher — not a household device: a P-256 key whose private half lives in Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/release-publisher`, readable only by the GitHub OIDC deploy role; its public key registered on the server as kind `release_publisher` for Meka's household, which may call only `/v1/releases/upload` and `/v1/releases/chunk` (every other route 403; unit-test that), and is listed in Activity as "GitHub build". The phone still checks the SHA-256 and Android still refuses anything not signed with the installed key; nothing installs without Meka's tap. Add a quiet "MEKA update ready" notification (low-importance channel, at most once per build) so he knows without opening the app. Keep `publish-fold.sh` working as the manual fallback. Infra test for the secret and role grant; backend tests for the publisher scope. Then tell Meka in the run summary that phone updates arrive on their own.
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-07 (core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the pipeline. **Infra:** Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/release-publisher` (the P-256 private key; a resource-policy Deny on GetSecretValue for every principal except `role/meka-os-github-deploy`, the service included) and `meka-os-dev/release-publisher-public` (`{"public_key": …}`, read by the service; env `MEKA_RELEASE_PUBLISHER_SECRET`), both `{}` until the workflow makes the pair (about $0.80/month together); infra test for the deny, no grant of the private key and the service's read. **Server:** `ReleasePublisher` (backend/ReleasePublisher.kt): not a household device and no bearer secret — `Authorization: Publisher github-build` plus the usual request signature, checked against the public key (re-read each minute); it publishes for the server's one household (refused if there are none or several) and may call only `/v1/releases/latest` (to see what is already published, which the plan's "chunk" became: downloading builds isn't the publisher's business) and `/v1/releases/upload`; every other route answers 403, no device can be called `github-build` (device ids are `[a-z0-9]`), and its builds are stored as published by `github-build`. Backend tests (ReleasePublisherTest: publish → the Fold fetches it, same build again, not newer, every other route 403 incl. chunk, another key / replay / wrong name / wrong route, no key set, two households). The same file has the publisher's client and commands (`backend publisher-keygen | publisher-latest | publisher-publish`, no database; the private key only from the environment). **Workflow:** a `publish-fold` job in deploy.yml after the server deploy (or its skip): skips with a notice when `MEKA_FOLD_SIGNING_KEY` isn't set; makes the publisher's key pair the first time (public half first); asks the server for the published build and skips with a notice unless `android/`, `core/`, `design/` or the build files changed since the commit that build was made from (main is linear: build N is the Nth commit); decodes the keystore into the runner's debug-key place, checks it opens with the debug defaults, builds `assembleDebug` (full history, so versionCode is the commit count), checks with apksigner that the APK carries that very certificate, publishes, and always deletes both keys from the runner. "Not newer" (e.g. the Mac published this commit first) is a notice, not a failure. **Fold:** a quiet "MEKA update ready" notification (`app_update` channel, low importance, "Build 412 · 24.3 MB · open MEKA to install") once per build and only while MEKA isn't on screen; Later or nothing on offer withdraws it; `AppUpdateRules.shouldNotify/told/notificationLine`, unit-tested. **Mac:** `tools/Copy phone signing key.command` copies its debug key as base64 and opens the repo's new-secret page (Needs Meka #14). `publish-fold.sh` is unchanged as the manual fallback.
  - *Live 2026-10-07:* CI green (run 37697302079); deploy run 37697539793 deployed the server, made the publisher's key pair and **published the first phone build from GitHub** (the signing secret was already set, and the APK's certificate matched the Mac's debug key).
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-07 (core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the GitHub build in Activity. When a build from the release-only publisher is complete, the server (`ReleaseActivity`, backend/ReleasePublisher.kt, through `Releases`' new `onPublished`) writes one `agent_action` entry of the new kind `PUBLISHED` into Meka's synced data as server-authored ops (ADR-008 addendum; fixed op ids, so once per build) and wakes the devices: "GitHub build published build 412" · "MEKA 0.1.412 · 24.3 MB · install it from Today on the Fold" · "Why: Hands-free phone updates · after a green CI run on main", nothing to undo, and the week line counts "1 phone build". Both apps' Activity panes show it as they show every entry (no app change); builds the Mac publishes by hand aren't listed. Core `ActivityRules.releaseId/releaseSummary/releaseFields`; entries of a kind an app doesn't know are skipped. Tests: ActivityTest (both devices, unknown kind), backend ReleaseActivityTest (listed once, re-publish writes nothing, next build a second entry, a Mac build or an incomplete one isn't listed, wake once). CI green (run 37699788994; macOS job at the next nightly run; the server redeploys).

- [x] Living Today — an always-on signature animation (Meka, 2026-10-08 21:15: "some work but too quick or subtle… I'd like a constant animation every time I open the app… you decide, don't make it look cheap"). Decided: a luxury-watch feel, built around the **Day ring**, gold on black; one ambient element, never several competing.
  1. **The Day ring is always on Today** (not only as an opening moment, and still after Shut down — then it shows tomorrow's first commitment): pinned under the greeting at a comfortable size (≈ 180 dp closed Fold, larger unfolded; Mac at the top of Today).
  2. **A sweeping gold second hand**: a fine brass hand with a soft comet tail orbits the ring once a minute, **smooth sweep (like a mechanical watch), not ticking**; on each new minute the "now" mark nudges forward with a tiny spring.
  3. **Breathing glow**: the ring's brass edge brightens and dims slowly (≈ 5 s cycle, 60 %→100 %), like a sleeping laptop light — calm, not flashing. At the top of each hour a single gentle shimmer runs round the ring.
  4. **The ring carries the day**: today's events are brass arcs on the ring (work hours as a faint band, gym/training highlighted), the current one glowing; tapping an arc opens the event; a running fast shows as an inner arc filling.
  5. **Every open is a moment, not once a day**: the ring draws itself in (≈ 900 ms Expressive, 500 ms Subtle) and the cards rise in a stagger each time Today comes to the front; the long first-of-day version adds the hour marks fading in one by one and the greeting letters lifting in.
  6. **Expressive must be obvious**: Expressive durations ×1.4 and larger travel (cards rise 24 dp, not 8 dp); Subtle = today's values; Off = still ring with no hand.
  7. Same on the bedside clock (larger ring, slower glow) and a mini ring on the cover screen. Mac: the same ring at the top of Today.
  8. Battery/quality: drawn on the GPU (Canvas, no recomposition per frame — `graphicsLayer` rotation, `drawWithCache`), 60 fps only while visible, paused when off screen or in power saving (then the hand jumps once a minute). Not added: coin flips, sparkles, bouncing icons (read cheap).
  Take right after the "Can't see the animations" item; verify on the emulator by sampling the hand angle and glow alpha across frames.

When on: the orb appears and listens for up to 6 s after Today opens; stops on silence; **skips when it's noisy** (measures the room for 300 ms first; above ~60 dBA it shows "Too noisy — tap to talk" instead); never when opened from a notification; a quiet chime marks listening.

- [~] **Nightly Mac build red (take first).** Dispatched `ci` run 37847660797 (2026-10-08 21:33 UTC, main at b75a901): core, android and tokens-and-infra green; the `macos` job (113552473230) fails to compile: `macos/MekaOS/CoreModel.swift:1444: type 'EventUndoOffer.Action' does not conform to protocol 'Equatable'` — the `case talk([AskUndo], [Int])` added in b7bfad2 (Talk to MEKA slice 3) carries `[any AskUndo]` (a Kotlin sealed interface, so a Swift protocol, not Equatable), which blocks the synthesised `==`. Fix: write `Action`'s `==` by hand (every other case as now; `talk` equal when the Ints match and the undos are the same objects, `zip` + `(a as AnyObject) === (b as AnyObject)`), or drop `Equatable` from `Action` if nothing compares it (check `EventUndoOffer`'s own `==` and the Mac tests). The push cap (70) was reached today, so this waits for the next UTC day; then push it, watch CI, and dispatch `ci` once to prove the macOS job green (it's the only failure; the job's log is readable with get_job_logs).
  - *Fix, 2026-10-09:* the job's full log (get_job_logs) shows this is the only compile error. `EventUndoOffer.Action` is no longer `Equatable` (nothing compares actions; the undo button switches on it), and `EventUndoOffer`'s `==` is written by hand: the same offer (its id and message), which is all the undo bar's `.animation(value:)` needs. Mac test `EventUndoOfferTests`. Dispatched `ci` run 37864821263 (core, android, tokens-and-infra green): the Equatable error is gone, and the compile then reached three strict-concurrency errors in `CoreModel.swift` — `sending 'history' / 'cards' / 'undos' risks causing data races` for Talk's `core.talk(history:)`, `core.doTalk(cards:)` and `undoTalk(undos:)`: an *array* of Kotlin objects is a stdlib type, so `@preconcurrency import MekaKit` doesn't quiet it the way it does a single Kotlin object. Each now crosses once through a `nonisolated(unsafe) let` copy (Kotlin values are immutable). That was the whole error list in the job's log. **Mac checked nightly:** today's one dispatch is spent, so the 21:00 UTC run proves the macOS job; if it is still red, fixing it comes first. Rule for Swift from here: never hand a `[KotlinType]` from the main actor to a core call directly.

- [~] Requests from my wife become tasks (Meka, 2026-10-09 09:21: "if my wife sends something like a task, or asks me to work from home on X date, and asks me to do something… this is the thing I keep forgetting"). Approved by Meka (he asked for it; the AI reads messages only from people he chooses). Build:
  1. **"Watch for requests from"** list in Work mode → People (default: the Family list; Meka adds his wife). Applies **all day**, not only at work. Official notification access only (WhatsApp, SMS/RCS, Samsung Messages; no WhatsApp libraries); 1:1 chats only by default (a switch for named groups).
  2. On each new message from a watched person, the Fold sends **only that message's text** (plus sender label and time; never contacts, numbers or other chats) to the AI layer with MEKA's tools schema; the AI returns zero or more **proposals**: task ("Pick up dry cleaning", due "tomorrow"), dated commitment ("Work from home Thu 15 Oct" → a Work-mode exception + calendar block), event ("Parents' evening Tue 18:00"), reminder ("Call your mum at 7"), or nothing. Untrusted content (ADR-006): never acted on automatically.
  3. **Needs you card**: "From Wife · 14:02 — 'can you pick up the dry cleaning tomorrow?'" → **Add task: Pick up dry cleaning · tomorrow** [Add] [Change] [Not a task]; dates resolved against Meka's calendar and work days; WFH requests propose switching that day's work mode to "Home" and blocking the day; duplicates merged (same request repeated). Quiet heads-up notification at Digest by default; a switch for "notify straight away" for the wife.
  4. **Never replies**, never marks read. Optional later: a one-tap "Done ✓ — reply 'Will do'" draft for approval (personal messages stay at "ask me").
  5. Limits shown in the screen honestly: only what Android's notification shows (very long messages may be cut; voice notes and photos can't be read — MEKA says "Wife sent a voice note" and offers a reminder to listen); messages read first on another device may not notify.
  6. Cost: one small AI call per message from watched people (pennies a month). Unit-test the proposal parsing and date resolution ("Thursday", "next Friday", "on the 15th") in core; Fold UI test for the card.
  Take right after the current Fold review items.
  - *Slice 1 done 2026-10-09 (core + server, no UI yet; core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* core `MessageRequestRules` (MessageRequests.kt; `MessageRequestsTest`): `shouldRead` (a message with text from the family list or the watch list, 1:1 unless its group is named), `worthAsking` / `isVoiceNote` / `voiceNoteProposal` (voice notes and bare photos never go to the AI; a voice note becomes "Listen to Wife's voice note"), `check` (known kinds only, a clean title, the day resolved again from the message's own when-words, never past or over a year ahead, WFH and events need a day, duplicates merged), `cards` (the Needs you card: "From Wife · 14:02", the quote, "Add task: Pick up dry cleaning · Tomorrow", "Work from home · Thu 15 Oct" with "Sets Thu 15 Oct to Home and blocks the day", Add · Change · Not a task, a spoken line; the same request again from the same person, or a re-post, makes no second card); `RequestDates.resolve` ("tomorrow", "Thursday", "next Friday", "on the 15th", "15 Oct", "Tue at 6pm"; Add event's reading plus "the 15th"). Wire `MessageRequestCodec` (`MessageRequestCodecTest`). Server `MessageRequestService` + `POST /v1/ai/message-request` (keyed devices; one tool-less SMALL call under `extract.message`, the message in a data block it can't close; nothing kept; `AiMessageRequestsTest`). ADR-006 addendum. Commit 87699e6, CI run 37913083380 green (first try).
  - *Slice 2 done 2026-10-09 (core only, no UI yet; Mac checked nightly):* the open cards as a synced store and the facade's read. Core `RequestCards` (RequestCards.kt; `RequestCardsTest`): new additive entity `request_card` (ADR-008 addendum; `resolved` TrueWins, the rest LWW, written once by the Fold) — `save(message, proposals)` (no second card for a re-post, for the same request from the same person while one is open, or for a card already answered), `open()` (oldest first, rebuilt each read so "Tomorrow" becomes "Today"; gone after 7 days or once its day has passed), `find`, `resolve(id, added · declined · changed)` (clears it on both devices and blanks the text); `MessageRequestRules.card` (one card, shared by `cards` and the store). Facade: `MekaCore.readRequest(item, lists, watching, groups)` → `RequestRead.Skipped` · `Read(newCards)` · `Unavailable(line)`: only a message `shouldRead` allows; a voice note becomes "Listen to Wife's voice note" with no AI; otherwise one message (text, sender's label, its time, today's date and Meka's work days) to `AiApi.messageRequest` (`POST /v1/ai/message-request`, signed; a server without it is "off"), the proposals checked against the day the message came, saved as cards; `MekaCore.requests` (StateFlow of the open cards) and `declineRequest(id)`. Tests: `MessageRequestFacadeTest`, `HttpSyncTransportTest` (the route). Gradle can't run in the build container, so CI is the facade's compile check. Commits 5378591 + 7e3a28e (a compile fix), CI run 37918280794 green.
  - *Slice 3 done 2026-10-09 (core + Fold; no Mac change, since only the phone listens; core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* (2) and (4). Core `RequestWatch` / `RequestWatchRules` (RequestWatch.kt; `RequestWatchTest`): the watch list (people beyond the Family list, named groups; names cleaned, never listed twice under another spelling, the Family list counts), `statusLine` ("Reading requests from Ada, mum and Wife · all day" · "Needs notification access to read requests" · "Add someone to watch for requests"), `groupsLine` ("1:1 chats only"), the hint and the honest `LIMITS` text, and `toRead` (one notification's messages that `shouldRead` allows, not already read, from the last 24 h, the newest 5) with `pruneSeen` (ids forgotten after 7 days). Fold: `CaptureStore` keeps the watch list and the read ids in its Keystore-sealed file (on the phone only, never synced); `WorkCaptureService` now parses notifications all day — the work-mode hold runs first as before (so urgent alerts never wait), then `readRequests` sends each new watched message through `MekaCore.readRequest` one at a time (a mutex, so a re-post never makes a second call); a message is marked read only once it was read or skipped, so offline it is tried again on the next re-post. Work mode gains **Watch for requests from** under Always notify (`RequestWatchSection`): the status line (cross-fades), Family listed as always watched, people added from contacts with Remove, the groups line, named groups with Remove and "Add a group" (a field unfolds in place with the expand spring, Done saves with a light haptic), and the limits. Gradle can't run in the build container, so CI is the Fold's compile check. Commit 24e2c71, CI run 37919263597 green (first try).
  - *Slice 4 done 2026-10-09 (core + Fold + Mac; Mac checked nightly):* (3)'s card on both apps. Core `RequestAcceptRules` / `RequestPlan` (RequestAccept.kt; `RequestAcceptTest`): Add on a task → a task on its day, planned at its time; a reminder → the same, reminding at its time while that is still ahead; an event → a calendar edit on the account Meka last added to (the usual five-second undo), or a planned task when no account allows editing (or the calendar refuses); work from home → that day becomes a **home day**: new additive `context_mode.workHomeDays` (ADR-008 addendum; LWW, past days dropped), so the work band on Today and the Calendar tab reads "Work from home 09:00–17:30" ("Working from home until 17:30" while on) on both apps (`WorkHours.homeDays` / `title`, `WorkBlock.title`, `WorkMode.setHomeDay`). Change → the proposal as a task (an event too), then it opens; work from home has no Change (`RequestCard.changeLabel` null). The card's WFH line now says what really happens ("Thu 15 Oct shows as work from home on both apps"; the earlier "blocks the day" isn't built). The undo bar's line (`doneLine`: "Added “Pick up dry cleaning” · Tomorrow", "Adding “Parents' evening” to Google · Tue 20 Oct · 18:00", "Thu 15 Oct: work from home"). Facade `acceptRequest` / `changeRequest` → `RequestDone`, `undoRequest` (takes the task, edit or home day back; the card stays answered) (`MessageRequestFacadeTest`, three new tests). Fold: `RequestCardView` in Needs you above the stack (and the open Fold's column), From · quote · action · detail · Add · Change · Not a task; Change springs Search up with the new task's detail (`RequestChangePane`); the Needs you badge and heading count requests; `RequestCardViewTest` (androidTest, 412 dp). Mac: `RequestCardsView` in Needs you and the wide window's column, `CoreModel.answerRequest` (Change opens the Search sheet on the task), the badge counts them; bridge `testRequestAnswersReachSwift`. Motion catalogue: new "Request cards (Needs you)" row. Commit d71a593, CI run 37921319780 green (first try).

  - *Remains:* a quiet Digest-level heads-up when the Fold reads a new request (a governor source from `RequestRead.Read`'s new cards, listed in Notifications) and "notify straight away" per watched person (a switch beside each name in Watch for requests from).

Small fixes: (1) **Watch face shows no event arcs** — at 10:48 on a work day the 09:00–17:30 work band and the 09:15 planned task should be arcs on the rim (next 12 hours); verify the arcs reach the closed-Fold face and are visible in both themes (brass 70 % for work band, accent for events). (2) **Calendar's work line is a short pill**; make it the same full-width band as Today's ("Work · Now · until 17:30" on today). (3) Today has a large empty gap between the now line and "1 done today" when nothing is left — tighten (or show "Nothing else planned · 6 h 40 free until 17:30" in that space). (4) Calendar legend: include Outlook/Hotmail when it has events in the window (check Outlook events are coming through at all — Meka's Hotmail calendar is connected; if it's simply empty, show "Hotmail · no events in the next 30 days" in Calendars instead of nothing).
- [ ] Messages assistant — all WhatsApp and texts (Meka, 2026-10-09 09:25; extends the item above, approved: "pay attention across WhatsApp… draft a reply I approve… actions to tasks… groups are noise, but I'd like to see what they said"). Build on the same capture:
  1. **Triage every incoming message** (official notification access; WhatsApp, SMS/RCS, Samsung Messages): the AI sorts each 1:1 message, and each group message that mentions Meka (@, his name "Meka"/"Chukwuemeka", or replies to him), into **Needs a reply** · **Action for you** · **FYI**. Group chatter without a mention → **Group digest**.
  2. **Needs a reply → drafted reply for approval** in Needs you: "Tunde: are you coming Saturday?" → draft "Yes, I'll be there — what time?" [Send] [Edit] [Not now]. Sending uses Android's own notification **Reply** action (RemoteInput — official, no WhatsApp libraries) while the notification is live; otherwise "Open chat" via WhatsApp's official click-to-chat link with the draft copied, Meka taps send. **Personal messages stay at "ask me": nothing is ever sent without Meka's tap**; never to family without his tap either; easy replies may be batch-approved ("Send all 3") like email.
  3. **Action for you → task proposal** (as in the item above: tasks, dated commitments, WFH days, events), with the sender and quote.
  4. **Group digest** at lunchtime and evening (and on demand in Needs you): one card per busy group — "Barça lads · 47 messages: lineup debate for Getafe; Tunde shared ticket link; plan for Saturday 7pm" — tap to expand the key messages; anything that needs Meka is promoted to Needs a reply/Action. MEKA clears the group's notifications after capturing them (`cancelNotification`) so groups stop buzzing; a per-group switch: Digest · Normal · Ignore.
  5. Work mode keeps its after-work summary; this replaces nothing, it adds triage all day.
  6. Privacy and cost shown in the screen: only message text + sender label go to the AI, one batched call per few minutes for groups, per message for 1:1; estimated a few pounds a month at most within the $40 cap; per-person/per-group "never send to AI" switch. Nothing stored on the server beyond the proposals; raw messages stay Keystore-sealed on the phone, dropped after 7 days.
  7. Limits stated: only what notifications show (long texts cut, voice notes/photos not readable — offered as "listen later" reminders), muted chats in WhatsApp send no notification so MEKA can't see them (setup screen explains the WhatsApp settings below), messages read first on another device may not notify.
  8. **Setup screen** (Work mode → Messages): checks notification access is on, explains WhatsApp → Settings → Notifications: keep notifications on; for Groups set Notification tone "None", Vibrate "Off", High-priority "Off" (MEKA then handles groups quietly) — **don't mute** groups in WhatsApp; Android Settings → Notifications → WhatsApp allowed.

- [x] Work mode + "while you were at work" summary (approved 2026-10-06; #10 approved 2026-10-07): work hours from a schedule plus a manual Work switch; Android notification listener captures WhatsApp, SMS and missed calls during work mode (official notification access only, no WhatsApp libraries); after work, one screen grouped by person with urgent items first ("urgent"/"emergency" breaks through immediately). Summaries are non-AI (grouping, ordering, counts) until the AI layer lands, then one line per person. Never marks anything read, never replies. Family list and an "always ring/always notify" list chosen from contacts. **Answers 2026-10-07:** work hours Mon–Fri 09:00–17:30 confirmed, **except UK bank holidays** (England and Wales list from gov.uk `https://www.gov.uk/bank-holidays.json`, fetched by the server weekly, cached on devices; work mode stays off on those days); Mac sync of the after-work summary approved (through Meka's own server, stored like his other data).

Fold: `WorkCaptureService` (official notification access) reads WhatsApp, the SMS apps and missed calls only while at work and keeps them in a Keystore-sealed file on the phone (never synced, dropped after 7 days); urgent messages and the always-notify list post a high-importance "Urgent while at work" alert; Work screen (switch, hours, notification access, alerts, Family and Always-notify picked from contacts); Needs you shows "N held for later" during work and the "While you were at work" card after, opening one screen grouped by person with an expandable thread; Done clears MEKA's copy only. Mac: "At work / Off work" in Today opens the Work sheet (switch and hours). Default hours Mon–Fri 09:00–17:30 until changed.
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-06 (Fold only; the summary itself is Fold-only until #10):* core `AfterWorkNudge` (nudge once on the change from at work to off work, only when something is held and the app isn't on screen; "Ada, Mum and 3 others · 1 urgent · 5 messages · 1 missed call"), unit-tested. Fold: `AfterWorkNudger` checks every work-mode change (clock tick, background sync — so a switch flipped on the Mac counts — the listener, process start) and records the last state, so it nudges once even if the phone was off when work ended; an inexact `setWindow` alarm (10-min window, no exact-alarm permission, ADR-007) wakes it at the end of work. One quiet "After-work summary" channel; names stay off the lock screen ("Your after-work summary is ready"). Tapping opens Needs you with the summary, which clears the nudge.

Server: `GovUkBankHolidays` (backend/integrations/Holidays.kt; GOV.UK's public `bank-holidays.json`, England and Wales, no key, nothing sent) behind a `HolidayProvider` interface; every household follows it like the fixtures and news feeds (a "GOV.UK · England and Wales" row, "Bank holidays · synced …", on the Calendars screen of both apps); refreshed weekly (retried at the next 5-minute poll after a failure; an unreadable or empty list leaves the last one in place), written into one `context_mode/bank_holidays` entity only when it changed (from 1 January last year on), which then wakes the devices (ADR-008 addendum). Core: `BankHolidays`/`HolidayCalendar`/`BankHolidayStore` and date-aware `WorkModeRules` (`scheduledOn`, `isWorkDay`): a shift starting on a bank holiday doesn't count (a night shift belongs to its start day), "next shift" skips holidays ("Off work · Christmas Day · next shift Tue 09:00"; more than a week away "Thu 14 Jan 09:00"), the Work switch still overrides; the brief and the shutdown's tomorrow preview say "Christmas Day · no work" and the evening shutdown starts at 18:00 that day. Everything that reads work mode (the Fold's capture, the after-work nudge, the bedside clock, both apps' Work lines) follows through `MekaCore`. Unit-tested (BankHolidaysTest incl. two-device sync, MekaCoreTest, backend HolidaysTest). CI green (run 37691566516; macOS job at the next nightly run; the core and server were touched, so the server redeploys and starts mirroring the list at its next poll).

Fold: the listener also holds each new item in the synced summary (and once at start, for what was held before); Needs you's card, the summary pane and the nudge read the synced summary; Done clears both ("Done clears MEKA's copy on the Fold and the Mac"). - [!] Call assistant for mobile calls at work (Needs Meka #9; code done, waiting on Needs Meka #13): during work mode, MEKA's call screening lets family, the always-ring list and repeat callers (2nd call within 3 min) ring; other calls are declined, and the carrier's "forward when busy" sends them to a cloud phone number where a voice assistant says it is Meka's assistant, that Meka is at work and will call back, takes a message, and asks if it's urgent. Messages land in the after-work summary; urgent ones alert Meka immediately. Fixed greeting written by Meka; the assistant always says it is an automated assistant; one switch turns it off. **Approved 2026-10-07** (paid phone service OK; greeting = "Hi, you've reached Meka's assistant. Meka is at work right now and will call you back. Can I take a message, and is it urgent?"). Provider: Twilio (UK number + voice). Build and test everything behind a provider interface first (call screening on the Fold, webhook + voice flow on the server, messages into the after-work summary, urgent → Critical alert); then add Needs Meka #13 for Meka to open the Twilio account, buy the UK number and set "forward when busy".
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-07 (core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* call screening on the Fold and the one switch. Core: `CallScreeningRules` (non-AI, pure; core/domain/CallScreening.kt): with the switch on and at work, family and the always-notify list (matched by phone number in any format) and repeat callers (a second call within 3 minutes, withheld numbers included) ring; everyone else is declined; off work or switched off every call rings. `PeopleLists` keeps each name's numbers (`nameForNumber`, `hasNumber`, `withNumber`, `pruned`). The switch is a new `callAssistant` field on `context_mode/work` (ADR-008 addendum, additive, LWW, off until Meka turns it on) carried on `WorkModeState.callAssistant`; `MekaCore.setCallAssistant`. Unit-tested (CallScreeningTest incl. two-device sync of the switch). Fold: `MekaCallScreeningService` (Android's call-screening role, asked for in the Work screen with "Let MEKA screen calls"): a declined call is rejected as busy (so the carrier's "forward when busy" takes it), still logged and shown as a missed call; anything slow or unexpected rings (3 s budget); the last few declined callers (number keys and times only) in private preferences. The Work screen gains a "Call assistant" section (status line cross-fades, the permission pill unfolds with the expand spring, tick haptic), and contacts are now picked with a phone number (no contacts permission), stored with the lists in the sealed file; names picked earlier say "No number · add again from contacts so their calls ring". Mac: a "Screen calls at work on the Fold" switch with the same status line in the Work sheet. CI green (run 37700864484; macOS job at the next nightly run; the core was touched, so the server redeploys and GitHub publishes the phone build).
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-08 (core and Mac touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the voice side. **Server** (backend/CallAssistant.kt): a provider-neutral `CallAssistant` flow behind `VoiceProvider` (ADR-009 row) with `TwilioVoice` as the adapter; routes `POST /v1/voice/{provider}/{incoming|recorded|answered|transcribed}`, each refused unless signed by the phone service over the exact public URL and form (`X-Twilio-Signature`; no device or publisher may call them; while the auth token is unset every webhook is refused). The call: if the one switch (`context_mode/work.callAssistant`, read server-side by an indexed latest-op query) is off, or the server has no single household, the call is turned away as busy; otherwise Meka's greeting ("Hi, you've reached Meka's automated assistant. Meka is at work right now and will call you back. Can I take a message, and is it urgent?"), a two-minute recording (beep, # or hang up to finish), then "Is it urgent? Press 1 or say yes…" (keypad or speech, en-GB), then goodbye; nothing recorded → "I didn't hear a message…" and nothing held. The message is written into the synced after-work summary as a server-authored `held_message` of the new kind `VOICE_MESSAGE` (ADR-008 addendum: one new field `urgent`, TrueWins; fixed op ids per call, so retried webhooks write nothing more); the transcript arrives later as its text (not after Done blanked it) and the recording is then deleted from Twilio. Urgent (the answer, or "urgent"/"emergency" in the transcript) → `Push.urgent`: every device woken at once at high FCM priority (still only `{"t":"sync"}`). **Infra:** Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/voice/twilio` (`{}` until Meka pastes `{"account_sid", "auth_token"}`; read-only grant; about $0.40/month), env `MEKA_VOICE_TWILIO_SECRET`; infra test. **Core:** `CallAssistantScript` (the fixed words), `CallAssistantRules` (held id, caller name incl. withheld/"ANONYMOUS", the urgent answer, transcript tidying, which voice messages alert), `CapturedItem.urgent/isUrgent/displayLine`, summary lines and the nudge count voice messages ("1 voice message · 1 missed call"), and a caller known only by number shows under their listed name on the Fold (ranked as family). **Fold:** an urgent voice message rings through once on "Urgent while at work" ("Voice message · Mum", its transcript or "Left a message with your call assistant and said it's urgent") after the sync the server's wake starts, within an hour of the call; the summary pane shows "Voice message · “…”". **Mac:** the after-work sheet shows the same line. Tests: core CallAssistantTest (both apps, names by number, alerts once, retries, Done), backend CallAssistantTest (the whole call over HTTP, retries, not urgent / no answer / emergency in the words, transcript after Done, switch off / no household / nothing said, signatures, tampering, unset token, recording deletion, high-priority wake), infra test. CI green (run 37702804322; macOS job at the next nightly run; infra and the server changed, so the stack and server redeploy and the phone build is published).
  - *Remaining:* Needs Meka #13 (the Twilio account, number and forwarding), then a live test call. *Check on the Fold:* whether a declined call posts Samsung's missed-call notification (the summary relies on it); if not, the screening service should hold the call itself.
  - *Low-balance guard (Meka, 2026-10-08, auto-recharge OFF by choice):* the server reads the Twilio balance daily (`GET /2010-04-01/Accounts/{sid}/Balance.json`, same secret) and MEKA shows a Needs you card "Call assistant credit low · £4.20 left · Top up" (opens twilio.com/console/billing) below £5, and a heads-up below £2. If the account is suspended or the balance is unreadable, the Fold stops declining calls during work (they ring as normal) so callers are never sent to a dead number; Work screen says "Call assistant paused · Twilio credit".
- [x] Repeating tasks and routines (daily/weekly/monthly/yearly; "every 2nd Tuesday"; skip/snooze one occurrence)
  - *2026-10-06 (Mac checked nightly):* core `Recurrence` (RRULE subset: every N days, weekly on chosen days incl. every other week, monthly on a date — the 31st falls on a short month's last day — or on the 1st–4th/last weekday, yearly incl. 29 Feb), `CivilDate` and a platform `LocalCalendar` so times of day survive the clock change; unit-tested. A repeating task is a series of occurrences: Done or Skip creates the next one with an id made from the series and day, so two devices finishing the same one offline end up with one next occurrence; missed ones aren't back-filled (an open one from an earlier day stays, marked "since Mon 5 Oct"). "Tomorrow" snoozes just this occurrence (or any one-off task) out of Today and the planner until its day. Steps on a repeating task make it a routine: each new occurrence brings them back unticked. Fold: Repeat row in the task detail unfolds the picker (Every day · Every weekday · Every Tue · Every 2 weeks on Tue · Monthly on the 6th · Monthly on the 1st Tue · Every year on 6 Oct), steps with spring ticks, and Done · Skip · Tomorrow · Delete; rows show "↻ Every weekday". Mac: the same as a Repeat menu, steps list and buttons.
  - *Later, if wanted:* a custom rule editor (e.g. "every 3 days" can be stored and shown, but the picker offers presets only); "after completion" repeats (next one counted from the day you finish).

Shared text is untrusted: it only ever becomes a task's title and notes. Fold: one capture sheet (springs up over whatever is on screen, field focused, "Kept in notes" preview, check pops and the sheet drops away; reduced motion fades) opened by the share sheet, "Capture in MEKA" on selected text, a quick-settings tile and a home-screen widget (pill + mic, follows the phone's light/dark). Voice is the phone's recogniser asked to stay on the device (`EXTRA_PREFER_OFFLINE`, no microphone permission in MEKA). Nothing saves until Add.
