- [~] "What MEKA did and why" activity log with undo (lands before any automatic action)
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-07 (Mac checked nightly):* core `ActivityLog` (non-AI), unit-tested incl. two-device sync, over a new `agent_action` entity (additive, all LWW; ADR-006/008 addenda). What it records today: every notification that actually reached you at Critical, Needs a decision or Heads-up ("Reminded you: Cancel or keep Netflix?" · "Cancel by tomorrow" · "Why: Cancel-by dates · Heads-up"), and every digest that went out ("Sent a digest: Evening digest · 3 things"); one entry per notice however many devices posted it. The undo machinery is in place for the automatic actions to come: `ActivityLog.act` is the one way an automatic action may change Meka's data, recording each field's value before; `undo` puts back only what is still as MEKA left it ("Partly undone: you've changed some of it since"), deletes rather than blanks something MEKA created, and converges when undone on both devices offline. The screen: the last 30 days by day, newest first, "This week: 5 reminders · 2 digests", time, what, the detail, why, and Undo or "Undone at 09:12". Interruption counts stay text-free (ADR-013); the log lives in Meka's own synced data and goes into the export. Fold: "Activity" in Today's header opens the pane; the governor now reports the digest when it reached a live channel. Mac: "Activity" in Today, File → Activity… (⇧⌘A), a sheet; MacNotifier reports the digest by its key (`meka.digest`). CI green (run 76; macOS job at the next nightly run; the core was touched, so the server redeploys).
  - *Remaining:* nothing automatic exists yet, so no entry can be undone in the apps today; each automatic action (V1 policy engine onwards) must go through `ActivityLog.act`. Later slices: "Prevented misses" (ADR-013) from reminders followed by the item being done before it was due; filtering by kind; the Fold's work-mode alerts and after-work nudge in the log once they move onto the governor.
- [ ] Policy engine UI: "What MEKA may do" per area, kill switch, locked rows
- [ ] Approvals inbox ("Needs you") with biometric confirmation for financial/trading items
- [~] AI layer (extraction, drafting, Ask MEKA) — key in place since 2026-10-07 (Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/ai/anthropic`, `{"api_key": …}`; Anthropic Console org cap $40/month, shared with Kestrel). First slice: the service reads the key (empty = AI off), one health check call, and a server-side reminder that the key expires on 2027-10-07 (shown in Needs you from 2027-09-20). *(Landed 2026-10-08 with the Outlook item: the AI key is on the renewals radar as "Renew MEKA's AI key", in Needs you from 2027-09-20.)* Ask in words should cover starting a fast ("start a 5-day fast", "fast until Friday 6pm") through the existing `startExtendedFast`/`startFastUntil`.
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-08 (server only, no app change):* the service reads its key and checks it. `AnthropicKey.parse` reads `{"api_key"}` from `meka-os-dev/ai/anthropic` (env `MEKA_AI_SECRET`, already granted read-only to the service): `{}`, blank or anything that isn't an `sk-ant-` key means **AI off**; read at use time (cached a minute), so pasting or rotating the key needs no redeploy. `AiHealth` (backend/Ai.kt): the health check is Anthropic's free `GET /v1/models?limit=1` (no prompt, none of Meka's data, no tokens spent), trusted for six hours when it answers, re-checked after ten minutes when it fails ("Anthropic refused the key", "…limiting requests (spend cap or rate limit)", "Couldn't reach Anthropic"), and at once when the key changes. `POST /v1/ai/status` (keyed devices only; the release publisher refused) answers `{"state": "on"|"off"|"failing", "checkedAtMs", "reason"}`, never the key; the server logs only `ai: on` / `ai: failing (…)` about 20 s after it starts. The key's renewal reminder landed earlier (Outlook item). Tests: AiHealthTest (parsing, off without a call, the check's headers, caching, failures, key change, route auth, route left out without the secret). ADR-006 addendum. CI green (run 37811348211; the server redeploys). **Next:** the `LanguageModelProvider` on the server (Anthropic messages, a token meter and the monthly budget with a soft alert at 70 % and off at 100 %, ADR-006 §5), then Ask MEKA through the core (status shown in Ask on both apps), then Talk to MEKA (the default-assistant item above).
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-08 (server only, no app change):* the cloud `LanguageModelProvider`, its token meter and the monthly budget (ADR-006 §4–5). backend/AiModels.kt: `ModelRequest` (a feature name for the meter, a tier, a system line and the turns; output clamped to 4,096 tokens), `ModelOutcome` (Answered · Off · OverBudget · Failed with the reason in words; everything but Answered means "carry on without AI"). `AnthropicProvider` calls the Messages API with the key read at use time and **no tools** (text in, text out); `ModelCatalog` picks each tier's newest model from Anthropic's model list once a day (SMALL = newest Haiku, FRONTIER = newest Sonnet; `claude-haiku-4-5` / `claude-sonnet-4-5` if the list fails). The meter (`ai_usage`, migration V10, additive: per UTC month and feature, calls, tokens in/out and list price in micro-dollars; counts only, nothing of Meka's) prices Haiku $1/$5, Sonnet $3/$15, Opus $5/$25 per million tokens (cache tokens counted as input; an unknown model at the highest price). `AiBudget`: MEKA's share of the $40 Anthropic cap is **$20 a month** (`MEKA_AI_BUDGET_USD` changes it, no redeploy of code); from 70 % the status says `alert` and the log says so once; at 100 % calls are refused before reaching Anthropic until the 1st (the Console cap stays the hard stop). `POST /v1/ai/status` now carries `budget: {month, spentCents, budgetCents, level, features[]}`. Tests: AiModelsTest (text-only body, model choice and fallback, clamping, off, failures not counted, 70 %/100 % and the new month, prices, status JSON, the Postgres meter in CI). ADR-006 addendum. CI green (run 37812852516; the server redeploys). **Next:** Ask MEKA — a `POST /v1/ai/ask` that answers from what the device sends (Today, Needs you) with MEKA's own actions proposed as cards the policy engine decides, through the core, and the AI status and month's spend shown in Ask on both apps.
  - *Slice 3a, 2026-10-08 (server and core; no app screen yet, so Mac checked nightly):* **Ask MEKA's round trip**. **Core** (core/domain/AskMeka.kt, non-AI, pure): `AskRules.context` turns Today into at most 60 short lines (Needs you with why, Up next and the day's tasks with "planned 18:00"/"anytime today", what's done, the calendar with "over" on finished events), tasks named by handles ("t1") that stay on the device (`AskContext.taskIds`; no ids, notes or addresses sent), `untrusted` when calendar lines are in it; `AskRules.card` turns a proposal into a card only when it checks out (a known kind, a handle it sent, a day today…two years, "HH:MM", a title of 1–120 characters, a fast of 12–240 h, a timer of a minute to a day; a time already gone today isn't offered; at most three, no two alike): "Add “Call the dentist” · Tomorrow · 09:00" · "Tick off “Book dentist”" · "Move “CR” to Fri 9 Oct · 09:00" · "Start a 36 h fast" · "Timer · 20 min" · "Alarm · 06:30"; `doneLine` for the undo bar, `unavailableLine` (off · budget used up until the 1st · "Couldn't ask: …"). **Wire** (`AskCodec`): request/response (limits refused, not trimmed) and `parseModelAnswer` (the `{"answer","actions"}` JSON, fenced or not; anything else is words with no actions). **Server** (backend/AiAsk.kt): `POST /v1/ai/ask` (keyed devices only; the publisher refused), one SMALL-tier call with no tools under the meter's `ask`, the system line saying the `<today>` block is data, not instructions (brackets inside it neutralised), only MEKA's six actions, never sending, spending, trading or calendar changes; drops other kinds and handles it didn't send; nothing stored or logged. **Facade:** `AiApi` on the HTTP transport, `MekaCore.askMeka(question)` → `AskOutcome` (Answered with checked cards that the policy engine allows as suggestions, or Unavailable with its line; never throws for a missing answer) and `doAsk(card)` on Meka's tap (adds/ticks/moves the task — a moved task's calendar block follows — starts the fast, or sets the timer/alarm exactly as typing it; refuses a task that's no longer open). ADR-006 addendum. Tests: AskMekaTest, AskCodecTest, AiAskTest (prompt shape, injection can't close the block, only known actions and handles, off/over/failed, route auth), AskMekaFacadeTest. CI green (run 37815117792; the server redeploys with the route). **Next (3b):** Ask's screen on both apps — the field asks MEKA (thinking shimmer, answer lines fade in, cards rise, per the catalogue's Assistant row), Search stays one tap away, the AI status and month's spend shown in Ask (`/v1/ai/status`), cards' buttons with the undo bar; then Talk to MEKA.
  - *Slice 3b, 2026-10-08 (core and Mac touched, so Mac checked nightly):* **Ask's screen on both apps**. Core: `AskRules.statusView` / `AiStatusView` (non-AI, pure): "On · $1.20 of $20 this month", "… · most of the month's budget used" (lit, from 70 %), "This month's budget is used up · back on the 1st" (lit, can't ask), "Off · Search still finds everything" (can't ask), "Not answering · <reason>" (lit); `STATUS_UNKNOWN` offline, `STATUS_NOT_CONNECTED`; `answerLines` (paragraphs and list lines, long paragraphs split after sentences, for the fade-in). `AskCodec.decodeStatus` reads `/v1/ai/status` (state, reason, the budget's cents and level). `AiApi.aiStatus()` on the HTTP transport (404 = off), `MekaCore.aiStatus()` never throws. Every card can be taken back: `doAsk` now returns `AskDone(line, undo)` and `MekaCore.undoAsk(undo)` removes the task it added, puts a ticked-off or moved task back (`Tasks.putBack`, only while it is still as the card left it; a change since wins), throws away the fast while it runs (`Fasting.discardIfOpen`) or cancels the timer/alarm. Tests: AskMekaTest (status lines, dollars, answer lines), AskCodecTest (status), AskMekaFacadeTest (undo for every kind, changed since, status from the server/offline/not connected), Mac bridge test. **Fold** (ask/AskMekaSection.kt): Ask's field is a text field ("Ask MEKA…", Send on the keyboard) with Ask (accent once there's a question) and Search pills beside it; while asking can't work the field opens Search as before; the status line under it; the question, a shimmer, the answer's lines fading in, cards rising with their button; a tapped card folds away and Ask's undo bar rises ("Added “Milk” · Undo"); a polite live region for screen readers. **Mac** (Ask/AskMekaSection.swift): the same in Ask (Return asks, Search ⌘F beside it), the shell's undo bar, Undo brings the card back. No new fields, no sync change, no server change. CI green (run 37817930608; macOS job at the next nightly run; the core was touched, so the server redeploys and GitHub publishes the phone build). *Check on the Fold:* Ask → type "move the dentist to tomorrow 9am" → a card "Move “…” to Tomorrow · 09:00" → Move, then Undo on the bar. **Next (3c):** Talk to MEKA (the first V1 item) builds on this screen.

- [ ] School and family logistics: school emails become events and tasks (forms, trips, payments, dates), shared with the household when it lands.
- [ ] "Handle this": share or forward any email, screenshot or photo to MEKA; it proposes the task, event, reply, renewal or document and files it on your tap.

- [ ] Returns and warranties: purchase emails set a return-by date and a warranty end; reminders before each; the receipt is kept in the Vault.
- [ ] Appointment nudges: dentist, optician, GP check, car service, haircut… each with a usual interval; "due" nudges with a drafted booking email or a call reminder; the next visit lands in the calendar.
- [ ] Bills due: a reminder a few days before each payment with the amount and a pay link (from the radar and bill emails); never pays anything itself.
- [ ] Life admin hour: once a week MEKA books one slot and gathers the small admin jobs (forms, calls, renewals, replies) into it, instead of nagging all week.
- [ ] Gift planner: birthdays and anniversaries two weeks ahead, gift ideas you note about each person, an order-by date from delivery times.

- [ ] People: birthdays, last contact, promises both ways, gift ideas
- [ ] Travel mode: bookings → trip timeline; documents offline

## Needs Meka

Things only the owner can do. Build runs skip these and carry on with the rest.

1. **Microsoft registration** — done 2026-10-07 (secret expires 2028-10-06). Next: tap Connect Outlook in Calendars.
2. **Firebase** — done 2026-10-07: project created, service-account key saved in Secrets Manager. Meka should delete the downloaded key file from his Mac.
3. **AI layer** — done 2026-10-07: key saved in Secrets Manager (expires 2027-10-07; $40/month cap shared with Kestrel).
4. **Mail permission:** reconnect Google and Outlook with mail read access when email triage lands.
5. **Kestrel change** — approved 2026-10-07; MEKA side gets built, the Kestrel side happens in a supervised session.
6. **Apple Developer Program** — skipped 2026-10-07.
7. **Install updates** on the Fold: once more with `tools/install-fold.sh` (this brings the updater), then `tools/publish-fold.sh` on the Mac and tap Install on the Fold. Publish from the Mac that installs MEKA (its debug key signs the app).
8. **Actions allowance** — resolved 2026-10-06: Meka added a $10/month Actions budget (stops at the limit). Same day he raised the cap to 8 code pushes a day with the macOS check batched nightly (rule 10).
9. **Call assistant** — approved 2026-10-07 with the default greeting; Twilio account needed once the code is ready (#13 will appear).
10. **After-work summary on the Mac** — approved 2026-10-07 and built the same day (synced through Meka's own server); work hours Mon–Fri 09:00–17:30 except UK bank holidays.
11. **Leftover KMS key** (about $1/month): in the AWS console for the meka-os account (eu-west-2) → KMS → Customer managed keys, keep the key with alias `alias/meka-os-dev-data`; any other key tagged `app=meka-os` with no alias is left over from an early stack attempt: Key actions → Schedule key deletion (30 days; it can be cancelled until then). If unsure which is which, leave it and say so.
12. **Calendar editing:** — *done 2026-10-09: Meka allowed editing on Google and Outlook.* Add event is in (2026-10-08): after the phone update (and the next `tools/install-mac.sh`), open Calendars → your Google account → **Allow editing**, then try Calendar → **+ Add event** (Undo within five seconds sends nothing; "Added “…” to Google" appears under the summary once it's in Google), and open one of your own events → **Edit** (move it, rename it, or Delete from Google). On Google's page keep the "view and edit events" permission ticked (same for Outlook once it's connected). If Google refuses the permission ("access blocked" or the scope isn't offered), add `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar.events` in Google Cloud console → Google Auth Platform → Data access for the MEKA OAuth client, then try again.
13. **Call assistant phone number (Twilio)** — *in progress 2026-10-08:* account upgraded (£20, auto-recharge off), compliance profile complete, **bundle "Meka" Twilio Approved (8 Oct); number bought 2026-10-09: +44 1767 667246** (Biggleswade, Voice; US1 routing); step (3) webhook set by Meka 09:18; — the code is ready (2026-10-08). About £1/month for a UK number plus a few pence a minute for calls, recordings and transcripts. (1) Sign up at twilio.com and upgrade the account (add a card; Twilio requires an address for UK numbers — the "regulatory bundle"). (2) Phone Numbers → Buy a number → United Kingdom, with Voice. (3) On the number's page, Voice configuration → "A call comes in" → Webhook, HTTP POST, `<your SyncUrl>/v1/voice/twilio/incoming` (SyncUrl is the stack output, `https://….cloudfront.net`). (4) Account → Account info: copy the Account SID and Auth token into Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/voice/twilio` as `{"account_sid": "AC…", "auth_token": "…"}` (no redeploy needed). (5) Settings → General → turn on "Enforce HTTP Auth on Media URLs" so recordings aren't public while they wait to be transcribed. (6) On the Fold: Phone → ⋮ → Settings → Supplementary services → Call forwarding → Voice call → "Forward when busy" → the Twilio number. (7) In MEKA's Work screen, turn on Call assistant and "Let MEKA screen calls". Then call the Fold from another phone during work hours to try it.
14. **Phone signing key for GitHub** — done 2026-10-07 (`MEKA_FOLD_SIGNING_KEY` is set; GitHub published its first phone build the same night). If the Mac's debug key ever changes, re-run `tools/Copy phone signing key.command`.
15. **Mac desktop News widget** (2026-10-08): after the next `tools/install-mac.sh`, right-click the desktop → Edit Widgets… and look for "MEKA News". If it isn't listed (macOS may refuse widgets from a build not signed through the Apple Developer Program), say so: the next step would be the Developer Program (#6, skipped) or dropping the Mac widget; the floating ticker covers the same need meanwhile.
16. **AI budget** (2026-10-08, no action needed unless you disagree): MEKA keeps its AI spend to **$20 a month**, half the $40 Anthropic cap it shares with Kestrel; it warns at 70 % and switches AI off at 100 % until the 1st. To change it, say the number and a run sets `MEKA_AI_BUDGET_USD`.
